197I. NOTIMCATI A2 No. 438. YANGTZE 0IE -IHN DITRICT. TEMPORARY BUOYAGE OF ITU ROOCKS. NOTICE is hereby given that a 4-bot Nun buoy, painted red and surmounted by a black diamond shape, has been placed to mark the It Middle Rock. From it the rock bears N. 850 E., distant 50 yards. Also that a similar buoy, painted red an ounted by a black spherical shape, has been placed to mark the Itu Lower Rock. From it the rock bears N. 53 distnt 2oo yards. These buoys will be maintained in position durng 1 river seaso All bearings given are magnetic. SHANGHAI, 6th M c
25TOMS GA . [JAN.-MAR., 1907.] NORH HANE ETRAC TO THE YANGTZE RIVE R. ATERATIONS IN AID TO NAVIGATION. NOTn is hereby given that the Aids to Navigation in theNorth Channel entrance to the Ynte River have been changed as follows:- Drinkwater Point Gas-lighted Buoy has been replaced in position and now bears 8. 871 W., distant 122 miles from Shaweishan Ligbthouse, and is moored in 18 feet at low water of spaing tides. Drinkwater Point Gas Light-vessel has been shifted and moored in 25 feet at low water of spring tides .4 E., dstan 346 miles from Drinkwater Point Beacon (erected on the site&#39;of D iwr Point Lighth6use). The Middle Islard Boy has been.remved, ada gas-lighted buoy, painted black, to b known s te Mddle Island G ted Bo, moored in 52 feet at low water of spring tides 44 W., istant es from the site of the Liuchiao Lighthouse. Liuchiao. Light ad Cliyao Bank Ga-ihe Bohave bieen discontinued. The Quarantine Anchorage Uppr tbeen repled by a gas-lighted buoy, painted red, and moore 0 fee water of spring tides . 42* W., distant t.i miles fromt the Quratin Beacon The Quarano Anchorage Lowe Buoy has bee replaced by a 6-foot conical buoy, painted re,and awoored in30 feet at low water of spring tides S. 121* E., distant 1.83 miles from the Qurantine Beacon. The Quarantine Buoys now mark the northern side of the channel as well as define the limits of the Quarantine Anchorage. All bearings given are magnetic. SANGouI, i ith March 1907. Ie
